### [1] [Open ultrasound foundation model for robust segmentation and clinical measurement across heterogeneous settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19230)
*Chao Qin,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan,Sarim Ather,Siddiq Anwar,Rao Muhammad Anwer,Shadab Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SonoCorpus（大规模超声数据集）和SonoBase（交互式分割基础模型），通过跨设备、操作者和解剖结构的广泛预训练，显著优于现有基线模型，具备极强的泛化能力和少样本适应能力，并开源了全部资源以推动社区发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管超声是全球应用最广泛的成像模式，但临床AI仍局限于狭窄的单任务模型，在面对设备、操作员或解剖结构变化时表现不佳，缺乏统一且鲁棒的基础模型。

Method: 构建包含53个公共数据集中456,963张图像和1,626,085个专家掩码的SonoCorpus资源；在此基础上预训练交互分割基础模型SonoBase；在15个引入新器官、设备、操作员和地理区域的数据集上进行评估，并与SAM2、MedSAM2、MedSAM3及专用模型进行对比；同时测试其在低资源环境下的适应性及少样本学习能力。

Result: SonoBase在所有评估数据集上均优于SAM2、MedSAM2和MedSAM3，并与同等数据训练的专用模型性能相当；在完全外部数据上，其精度超过基线模型在其自身分布内基准上的表现；其衍生的射血分数误差在观察者间变异范围内，误分类率更低；胎儿头围和孕龄误差低于观察者间变异水平；在基线完全失败的情况下，SonoBase能恢复可用分割的比例高达81%；仅需5个标注示例即可适应新场景，且优势源于超声特定预训练而非单一架构。

Conclusion: SonoBase通过大规模、多样化的超声数据预训练，实现了强大的泛化能力和鲁棒性，能够克服传统单任务模型的局限性，为临床超声AI提供了可靠的基础平台，其全面开源的策略有助于促进可重复研究和社区创新。

Abstract: Ultrasound is the most widely deployed imaging modality worldwide, yet clinical AI remains fragmented into narrow single-task models that fail when device, operator, or anatomy changes. Here we present SonoCorpus, an open resource unifying 456,963 images and 1,626,085 expert masks from 53 public datasets spanning 24 clinical applications and 17 countries, and SonoBase, an interactive segmentation foundation model pretrained on it. Across fifteen evaluation datasets introducing new organs, devices, operators, and geographies, SonoBase outperforms SAM2, MedSAM2, and the concept-promptable MedSAM3 on every dataset and matches per-dataset specialist models trained on the same data; on fully external data it exceeds the accuracy these baselines achieve on their own in-distribution benchmarks. Ejection fraction derived from its segmentations falls within inter-observer variability (6.63\% error), with fewer misclassifications at the defibrillator-candidacy threshold than either promptable baseline (13\% versus 18--42\%); fetal head-circumference (1.81~mm) and gestational-age (1.2 days) errors fall below inter-observer variability. Where a baseline fails outright, one in four test cases, SonoBase recovers a usable segmentation in 81\% of them, including on handheld probes operated by minimally trained users in two low- and middle-income countries (Sierra Leone and Tanzania). Five labeled examples can help the model adapt to a new setting, and the identical training protocol transfers well to newer models such as SAM3, locating the advantage in ultrasound-specific pretraining rather than any single architecture. To ensure reproducibility and enable the community to build on SonoBase as a platform, we release all checkpoints, optimizer states, data-split indices, deduplication hashes, and starter code.

</details>


### [2] [RAUL: Reference-Assisted Ureteroscopy Localization for Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19236)
*Fangjie Li,Mai Bui,Charan Mohan,Michael Miga,Matthieu Chabanas,Nicholas Kavoussi,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RAUL框架，通过参考视频辅助从输尿管镜视频中恢复轨迹，无需外部传感器即可量化外科医生技能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决输尿管镜手术中因解剖导航不完整导致的重复干预问题；目前缺乏客观指标来量化操作者技能水平及培训进度。

Method: 构建RAUL（参考辅助重建）框架，利用高质量参考视频生成基准，将后续探索视频与之匹配定位；基于电磁追踪数据验证精度，并计算导航指标以比较不同经验水平的受训者。

Result: 平均平移均方根误差为0.5±0.1 mm；相比标准运动恢复结构（SfM），帧级定位覆盖率从50.5%提升至86.1%；重构轨迹显著区分了高、低经验受训者的导航表现。

Conclusion: RAUL实现了比传统SfM更完整的轨迹恢复，支持无额外跟踪设备的轨迹驱动技能评估，为可扩展的自动化的输尿管镜导航技能评估提供了首个视频-only解决方案。

Abstract: Objective: Incomplete navigation of anatomy during ureteroscopic kidney stone surgeries can contribute to repeat interventions. While skilled surgeons have lower reintervention rates, there are no objective metrics to quantify scope-navigation performance to evaluate when a trainee becomes skilled. This work aims to recover ureteroscope trajectories from endoscopic video and derive navigation metrics to quantify differences in skill. Methods: We propose RAUL, a reference-assisted reconstruction framework for recovering ureteroscope trajectories from ureteroscope videos only in phantoms. For each phantom, we use a slow, high-quality reference exploration video to generate a reference reconstruction. We localize subsequent exploration videos against this reference. We evaluate localization accuracy against electromagnetically tracked scope pose. We compute navigation metrics from phantom exploration trajectories to compare surgical residents across experience levels. Results: The proposed reference-assisted framework achieves a mean translation root mean square error of $0.5 \pm 0.1$ mm across 9 phantoms. Compared to standard Structure-from-Motion (SfM), the proposed pipeline increases frame-wise localization coverage from $50.5 \pm 14.9\%$ to $86.1 \pm 7.2\%$ of all video frames. The reconstructed trajectories revealed significant differences between high- and low-experience trainees in established navigation metrics. Conclusion: RAUL enables substantially more complete recovery of ureteroscope trajectories from videos compared to standard SfM pipelines, enabling trajectory-based skill assessment without additional tracking equipment. Significance: To the best of our knowledge, this is the first use of video-only recovery of ureteroscope trajectories without external tracking sensors for skill assessment, supporting scalable automated assessment of ureteroscopy navigation skill.

</details>


### [3] [Can Vision-Language Models Judge Olympic Diving? From Reasoning to Scores in Zero-Shot Action Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19354)
*Henry O. Velesaca,David Freire-Obregon,Luigi Miranda,Abel Reyes-Angulo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了开源视觉语言模型（VLMs）在奥运跳水视频零样本动作质量评估（AQA）中的能力。通过提出一种结合TF-IDF、降维和集成学习的回归框架，利用VLMs的语义推理和阶段子评分，最终实现了0.67的Spearman相关系数，显著优于单一模型表现，证明VLMs可作为可解释性体育评估辅助工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决奥运项目中因人体运动复杂性和裁判主观性导致的自动化动作质量评估难题，探索开源VLMs在该领域的潜力。

Method: 基于AQA-7基准数据集，提出一种回归框架：利用VLMs生成语义推理和阶段级子评分，结合TF-IDF向量化、维度约简和集成学习预测最终得分。

Result: 独立VLMs的Spearman相关性低于0.32；提出的四模型集成回归框架将相关性提升至0.67；文本推理特征的表现持续优于原始数值子评分。

Conclusion: VLMs生成的解释包含丰富信息，具有作为可解释、半自动体育性能评估辅助工具的巨大潜力。代码已公开。

Abstract: Automated action quality assessment (AQA) in Olympic sports remains a challenging task due to the complexity of human motion and the subjectivity inherent in expert judging. This work evaluates the capability of open-source Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to perform zero-shot action quality assessment on Olympic diving videos using the AQA-7 benchmark dataset. In this regard, a regression-based framework is pro-posed to leverage both the semantic reasoning and phase-level sub-scores generated by the VLMs, combining TF-IDF vectorization, dimensionality reduction, and ensemble learning to predict final competition scores. Experimental results show that standalone VLMs achieve moderate Spearman correlations below 0.32, while the proposed ensemble regression framework substantially improves performance in the reported evaluation, reaching a Spearman correlation of 0.67 with a four-model configuration. Textual reasoning features con-sistently outperformed raw numerical sub-scores, highlighting the richness of VLM-generated explanations for action quality analysis. These findings suggest that VLMs hold strong potential as assistive tools for explainable and semi-automated sports performance evaluation. The code is publicly available on GitHub https://github.com/hvelesaca/olympic diving judge vlm

</details>


### [4] [Open-vocabulary 3D object detection with promptable segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19358)
*Ömer Faruk Deniz,Mustafa Taha Koçyiğit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、支持开放词汇的3D物体检测方法。通过提示分割模型（SAM3）生成2D实例掩码，并结合场景几何或监督学习的3D边界框构建方法，在nuScenes数据集上实现了具有竞争力的检测性能，证明了无需标注数据即可实现高效3D检测的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶3D目标检测器依赖于大规模人工标注的3D框数据进行训练，导致其只能识别固定的类别列表，无法处理未见过的类别（即“闭集”问题）。本文旨在探索是否可以在无需训练且支持开放词汇（open-vocabulary）的情况下解决这一任务。

Method: 1. 使用提示分割模型（SAM3），以类别名称作为文本提示，在车辆的六个环绕视场相机中获取实例掩码。
2. 将2D掩码转换为度量级3D边界框，核心实验在于控制变量：固定2D检测结果，仅改变3D几何信息的来源。
3. 三种几何来源策略：
   - 仅从图像预测几何结构；
   - 基于原始LiDAR点云，利用无规则拟合掩码内的框（无需训练）；
   - 在推理时借用监督学习的边界框几何信息。
4. 反向应用：利用由掩码构建的“三状态相机见证规则”增强监督LiDAR-only检测器。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上的评估结果如下：
- 仅图像几何：mAP为0.183。
- 无训练LiDAR点云拟合：mAP为0.298，NDS为0.348（零标注成本）。
- 借用监督几何信息：mAP提升至0.413，NDS提升至0.555。
- 增强监督LiDAR检测器：mAP从0.596提升至0.630。
- SAM3覆盖分析：能发现84%范围内正确命名的对象。失败案例主要源于类别命名错误或几何形状难以适应，而非完全未见类别。

Conclusion: 该流水线的主要瓶颈在于测量精度而非2D检测能力。类别混淆和置信度校准问题依然存在。研究证明，通过结合开放词汇分割模型与几何推断，可以在无需额外标注数据的情况下显著提升3D检测性能，为免训练、开放词汇的自动驾驶感知提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Three-dimensional object detection for autonomous driving is dominated by detectors trained on large corpora of human-annotated 3D boxes. Such a detector learns a fixed category list, and everything outside it is invisible. This paper asks whether the task can be solved training-free and open-vocabulary. A promptable segmentation model (SAM3), queried with class names as text prompts, supplies instance masks in the vehicle's six surround-view cameras, and the masks are turned into metric 3D boxes using the geometry of the scene. The core is a controlled three-stage comparison on nuScenes in which 2D detection is held fixed and only the source of 3D geometry changes. Geometry predicted from images alone reaches 0.183 mean average precision (mAP) under the official protocol; fitting boxes from raw LiDAR points inside the same masks with training-free rules reaches 0.298 mAP / 0.348 nuScenes detection score (NDS) at zero labeling cost; borrowing supervised box geometry at inference time lifts the same detections to 0.413 mAP / 0.555 NDS, which locates the pipeline's largest deficit in measurement precision rather than 2D detection, while class confusion and confidence calibration survive that substitution. Reversing the direction, a three-state camera-witness rule built from the same masks improves a supervised LiDAR-only detector from 0.596 to 0.630 mAP, roughly half the gain of fully supervised camera fusion, with no training. A coverage analysis shows that SAM3 finds 84% of in-range objects with a correctly named mask; the classes that fail in the official metric are misnamed or geometrically unforgiving, not unseen.

</details>


### [5] [LinePilot Digitizer: Line-Plot Recovery with Manual and Automatic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19377)
*Fengbo Ma,Rayan Akhtar,Aakash H. Joshi,Xiaoting Li,Haijian Sun,Zhen Xiang,Xianyan Chen,Yiping Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LinePilot数字化工具及DigitizerBench基准，通过三种校准模式实现从折线图中高精度恢复数值序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从折线图中准确进行坐标轴校准和可靠曲线提取的难题，填补该领域系统性评估基准的空白。

Method: 开发LinePilot（含标准、增强、OCR三种校准模式）结合连续颜色曲线恢复技术；构建首个专用基准DigitizerBench，采用正交设计涵盖信号、渲染和图表结构因素，并使用FPC-NRMSE指标进行评估。

Result: 在DigitizerBench-Full上，LinePilot (OCR)表现最佳（最低平均FPC-NRMSE 0.672，最高可信可用性38.2%）；在DigitizerBench-Lite上，LinePilot (enhanced)表现最优（最低平均FPC-NRMSE 0.081，100%输出成功率，93.3%可信可用性）。

Conclusion: 三种校准模式在共享曲线恢复工作流中提供了自动化程度、用户控制与准确性之间的实用权衡，正交基准设计有助于识别影响性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Recovering numerical series from line plots requires accurate axis calibration and reliable curve extraction. We present LinePilot Digitizer (LinePilot), which combines continuous color-based curve recovery with three calibration modes: LinePilot (standard), LinePilot (enhanced), and LinePilot (OCR). We also introduce DigitizerBench, the first dedicated benchmark for systematically evaluating digitizer performance, using an orthogonal design spanning signal, rendering, and plot-structure factors with complementary automatic and human-guided evaluations. We evaluate performance using failure-penalized capped normalized root-mean-square error (FPC-NRMSE), which assigns unit loss to missing, unusable, or catastrophically inaccurate outputs. On DigitizerBench-Full, LinePilot (OCR) achieves the lowest mean FPC-NRMSE (0.672) and highest trusted usability (38.2%) among the tested automatic pipelines. On DigitizerBench-Lite, LinePilot (enhanced) achieves the lowest mean FPC-NRMSE (0.081), 100% output success, and highest trusted usability (93.3%). The orthogonal benchmark design further enables factor analysis to identify the factors that most significantly affect digitizer performance. Together, the three calibration modes provide a practical trade-off between automation, user control, and accuracy within a shared curve-recovery workflow.

</details>


### [6] [Riemannian--Lorentz Fusion of Vision Transformers and State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19384)
*Badri N. Patro,Vijay S. Agneeswaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RLPF的异构模型融合方法，通过黎曼-洛伦兹参数融合技术，将不同架构（ViT与SSM）的对齐参数组映射到双曲空间计算测地线重心，实现了优于父模型的混合精度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度学习扩展中的数据耗尽、训练成本指数级增长及资源集中问题；现有权重空间融合方法难以处理架构和参数形状不同的独立训练视觉模型（如ViT与SSM）。

Method: 提出Riemannian-Lorentz Parameter Fusion (RLPF)：1. 按语义角色对齐参数组；2. 将对齐组投影至共同坐标系；3. 将选定坐标提升至高曲率的双曲空间（Lorentz超曲面模型）；4. 计算正则化测地线质心并解码回两个分支；5. 使用学习到的门控机制结合分支 logits。固定曲率用于组件组，归一化参数视为欧几里得空间。

Result: 微调后系统在CIFAR-10上达82.37%，Oxford-IIIT Pet上达75.04%，ImageNet-1K上达78.58% top-1准确率，均显著高于对应最佳父模型准确率。预微调初始化在ImageNet-1K上已达77.80%。

Conclusion: 几何感知的异构融合具有研究价值，但RLPF并非无训练的单检查点融合方法，而是涉及门控和最终模型训练的双分支混合架构。

Abstract: Scaling deep learning faces critical bottlenecks: data exhaustion, exponential training costs, and resource concentration. Model merging combines pre-trained checkpoints without gradient descent, offering orders-of-magnitude savings versus retraining. Combining independently trained vision models is difficult when their architectures and parameter shapes differ. Existing weight-space merging methods generally assume aligned, shape-compatible checkpoints, whereas a Vision Transformer (ViT) and a state-space model (SSM) implement token mixing with different operators. We study a hybrid Heterogeneous merging setting that retains both architectures while aligning parameter groups by semantic role. Our proposed Riemannian--Lorentz Parameter Fusion (RLPF) method projects aligned groups to common coordinates, lifts selected coordinates to the Lorentz hyperboloid model of hyperbolic space, computes a regularized geodesic barycenter, and decodes the result into the two branches. A learned gate then combines branch logits for each input. Component groups use fixed curvature values, with normalization parameters treated as Euclidean. In the results available in this manuscript, the fine-tuned system obtains 82.37\% on CIFAR-10, 75.04\% on Oxford-IIIT Pet, and 78.58\% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K; the corresponding best-parent accuracies are 76.54\%, 71.42\%, and 76.42\%. On ImageNet-1K, the reported pre-fine-tuning initialization reaches 77.80\%. These results support further study of geometry-aware heterogeneous fusion, but not a training-free single-checkpoint merge: RLPF is a two-branch hybrid whose gate and reported final models are trained.

</details>


### [7] [WZPlanner: Safe End-to-End Path Planning for Autonomous Driving in Work Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19393)
*Nishad Sahu,Changzhong Qian,Guangzhou Cai,Shounak Sural,Ragunathan,Rajkumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WorkZonePlan数据集和WAVE生成管道，并引入BoundaryFormer (BF) 及改进版 BF++ 模型，通过联合预测车道线、施工区边界和驾驶轨迹，显著提升自动驾驶车辆在施工区域的感知与规划性能，且模型更轻量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决施工区域因临时交通管制和封闭导致车道几何形状改变，且现有车载地图缺失这些信息，从而挑战自动驾驶（AV）感知和规划的问题；同时缓解缺乏带有结构化几何监督的公开数据集导致的泛化能力受限问题。

Method: 1. **数据集构建**：发布 WorkZonePlan 数据集，包含14.9万+合成样本和5千+真实世界多模态样本，提供3D标注（车道边界、施工区边界、驾驶轨迹选项），并提供76个闭环CARLA场景回放。
2. **数据生成管道**：提出 WAVE，一种用于在虚拟和真实环境中创建数据集的半自动化管道。
3. **模型架构**：提出 BoundaryFormer (BF)，基于Transformer的模型，使用槽注意力（slot attention）联合预测车道和施工区边界多项式及驾驶轨迹。
4. **模型优化**：提出 BF++，包含相机和相机+激光雷达变体，采用度量地面平面编码、类型化的边界/轨迹查询、长程点锚点、图像空间曲线细化以及保守的门控激光雷达融合技术。

Result: 在211条通用评估路线上，BF++-Camera 和 BF++-Camera+LiDAR 分别取得 63.0 和 64.4 的驾驶得分（Driving Scores），显著优于 SimLingo (59.3) 和 TransFuser++ (26.1)。同时，BF++ 的模型规模比 SimLingo 小40倍，比 TF++ 小10倍以上。

Conclusion: 联合预测车道边界、施工区边界和驾驶轨迹是提升施工区域自动驾驶安全性的有前景的方向。

Abstract: Work zones alter lane geometry through temporary traffic controls and closures that may be absent from on-board maps, challenging autonomous vehicle (AV) perception and planning. Generalization is also limited by scarce public datasets with structured geometric supervision. We present WorkZonePlan, a dataset comprising 149K+ synthetic and 5K+ real-world multimodal samples with 3D annotations for lane boundaries, work zone boundaries, and driving trajectory options. It also provides 76 closed-loop CARLA scenarios replayed under three weather conditions, yielding 228 Bench2Drive-format evaluation routes. We introduce WAVE (Work-zone-focused AV data generation in Virtual and rEal Environments), a semi-automated pipeline for creating the dataset, and BoundaryFormer (BF), a transformer-based model that jointly predicts lane and work zone boundary polynomials and driving trajectories. BF uses slot attention for boundary prediction. Ablations show that a separate trajectory decoder using boundary slot features substantially improves trajectory prediction over a slot-attention-only approach. Building on this finding, BF++ offers Camera and Camera+LiDAR variants with metric ground-plane encoding, typed boundary/trajectory queries, long-range point anchors, image-space curve refinement, and conservative gated LiDAR fusion. On the 211 routes common to all four models at the evaluation freeze, BF++-Camera and BF++-Camera+LiDAR achieve Driving Scores of 63.0 and 64.4, respectively, compared with 59.3 for SimLingo and 26.1 for TransFuser++ (TF++). BF++ is 40 times smaller than SimLingo and more than 10 times smaller than TF++, while achieving higher Driving Scores. These results support jointly predicting lane boundaries, work zone boundaries, and driving trajectories as a promising direction toward safer AV operation in work zones. Code and dataset: https://github.com/Nishad-Sahu/WZPlanner.

</details>


### [8] [RGS: Reflection-aware Gaussian Splatting via Learning Geometry Continuity for Reflective Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19421)
*Xiaobiao Du,Yida Wang,Cheng Bi,Kun Zhan,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为RGS的物理基础延迟渲染框架，通过利用3D基础模型的几何先验和跨视图形状一致性正则化解决反射区域表面坍塌问题，显著提升新视角合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）方法在反射区域常出现表面坍塌问题，导致几何结构不准确且镜面反射质量低下。

Method: 提出Reflection-aware Gaussian Splatting (RGS)：1. 利用强大的3D基础模型提供几何先验；2. 引入跨视图形状一致性正则化以优化几何表面，减少空洞；3. 设计感知反射的密度增加策略以捕捉多视角下的镜面变化。

Result: RGS能在反射区域生成更平滑的几何表面并减少几何空洞，能够高质量渲染具有反射特性的物体，实验表明该方法达到最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 通过结合3D基础模型先验与特定的正则化及密度增加策略，RGS有效解决了3DGS在反射区域的缺陷，显著提升了新视角合成的几何质量和渲染效果。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has significantly improved the quality of novel view synthesis with explicit Gaussian representation. However, we observed that existing 3D Gaussian Splatting methods (3DGS) often suffer from surface collapse issues on reflective regions, and thus produce inferior geometry and low-quality specular. In this work, we propose a physically-based deferred rendering framework, named Reflection-aware Gaussian Splatting (RGS), that can accurately model specular regions and improve novel view synthesis performance. Specifically, we found that a powerful 3D foundation model can provide a strong 3D geometric prior to foster correct geometric modeling. Based on this, we propose a cross-view shape consistency regularization to regularize the geometry surface with the large model prior and cross-view constraints. In this manner, our RGS can produce smoother geometric surfaces on reflective regions while reducing geometric hollows. To further improve rendering results on reflective regions, we present a reflection-aware densification strategy that is designed to capture specular variations across various views. With this strategy, our RGS is able to render novel views of objects in higher quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate our method consistently renders high-quality reflective objects, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [9] [Seeing Abnormal from Normal: Glomerular Abnormality in Representations of Normal Renal Morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19444)
*Greta Hasko,Rachit Saluja,Tianyu Shi,Leiyue Zhao,Yuechen Yang,Daniel Reisenbuechler,Tianyuan Yao,Zhenhao Guo,John Cannon,Haichun Yang,Yuankai Huo,Yuling Chi,Lorraine Gudas,Mert R. Sabuncu,Yihe Yang,Ruining Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NoRDeC框架，利用仅含正常数据的冻结U-Net骨干网络进行肾小球异常检测与表征分析，无需异常标签即可训练，在多项指标上优于PaDiM和PatchCore。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度肾小球病理评估需区分多种罕见或罕见的亚型（如全球性/节段性硬化、缺血性等），监督分类因缺乏各类别标注数据而难以实施；单类异常检测可通过建模正常数据来发现未见过的异常，解决标注稀缺问题。

Method: 基于Omni-Seg预训练的冻结残差U-Net骨干网络提取特征；提出NoRDeC框架，结合马氏距离正常参考评分与逐层表征分析；使用两个机构的肾小球图像数据，评估不同层级与聚合策略，并与PaDiM、PatchCore对比；采用中心化核对齐（CKA）分析表征关系。

Result: Layer 4配合Center-70聚合策略达到最高平均AUROC（0.926±0.013）；NoRDeC在七种异常类别中的六种及总体分析中表现最佳；CKA分析显示异常类型依赖的层间关系变化未被异常分数完全捕捉；模型仅用正常数据拟合，异常标签仅用于配置选择和评估。

Conclusion: 冻结的肾部特征提取器可在不使用异常示例的情况下，同时支持肾小球异常的检测和表征级 characterization，证明了无监督异常检测在病理细分中的有效性。

Abstract: Fine-grained evaluation of glomerular pathology must distinguish normal glomeruli from abnormalities such as global and segmental glomerulosclerosis, obsolescent, ischemic, solidified, disappearing, and atubular glomeruli. Supervised classification requires labeled examples of every category, which is impractical when subtypes are rare or absent from the training cohort. One-class anomaly detection offers an alternative by modeling normal data and scoring deviations, allowing previously unseen abnormalities to be detected. We use the frozen residual U-Net backbone of Omni-Seg, pretrained to segment structurally normal renal primitives without abnormal-subtype labels. We propose NoRDeC (Normal-Reference Detection and Characterization), a framework combining Mahalanobis normal-reference scoring with layer-wise representation analysis to determine whether and where glomerular pathology is encoded, how spatial aggregation affects detection, and whether abnormalities alter inter-layer relationships differently. Using glomerular images from two institutions, we evaluate backbone layers and aggregation strategies, compare NoRDeC with PaDiM and PatchCore, and analyze representations using centered kernel alignment (CKA). Layer 4 with Center-70 aggregation achieved a pooled AUROC of $0.926\pm0.013$. NoRDeC achieved the highest AUROC in six of seven abnormality categories and in the pooled analysis, while CKA suggested subtype-dependent changes in inter-layer relationships not captured by anomaly scores alone. The normal-reference model is fitted using only normal glomeruli; abnormality labels are used for configuration selection, evaluation, and grouping in the representation analysis. These results show that a frozen renal feature extractor can support both detection and representation-level characterization of glomerular abnormalities without using abnormal examples to fit the detector.

</details>


### [10] [Efficient Unified Multimodal Understanding (EUMU): Winning Solution for the MUMU Track at the 8th LSVOS Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19451)
*Dayoung Kil,Seong-heum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EUMU是LSVOS挑战赛MUMU赛道冠军方案，通过共享视觉特征和任务感知推理优化，在单一高效模型中联合实现图像标签、开放词汇目标检测和图像描述生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决移动设备上对单一高效模型的需求，该模型需同时执行多概念图像标签、开放词汇目标检测和图像描述生成三项任务，并满足严格的资源约束。

Method: 基于预训练多模态模型，利用其提示能力进行检测和描述；在共享视觉特征上训练轻量级头以预测质量、场景和事件标签。引入任务感知推理细化机制，复用任务输出作为跨任务线索：检测线索辅助描述生成，描述线索辅助恢复漏检对象，图像统计及跨任务线索优化标签预测。

Result: 模型参数量239.169M，计算量23.947 GFLOPs，峰值推理内存4.5 GB，最终挑战得分17.3409，成功平衡了性能与资源效率。

Conclusion: EUMU证明了通过任务间线索交互和轻量化设计，可以在单一模型中有效统一多种多模态理解任务，为资源受限环境下的多任务处理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The Mobile Unified Multimodal Understanding (MUMU) Challenge requires a single efficient model to jointly perform multi-concept image tagging, open-vocabulary object detection, and image captioning. We present Efficient Unified Multimodal Understanding (EUMU), the winning solution for the MUMU Track of the 8th LSVOS Challenge. EUMU builds on a shared pretrained multimodal model, using its prompt-based capabilities for detection and captioning and training lightweight heads on shared visual features to predict quality, scene, and event tags. Rather than treating the three tasks independently, EUMU applies task-aware inference refinement by reusing task outputs as cross-task cues. For detection, caption cues help recover objects missed by the initial detection. For captioning, detection cues help refine the caption to better reflect the detected objects. For tagging, image statistics refine quality predictions, while caption and detection cues refine scene and event predictions. This design unifies all three tasks within a single model while satisfying the challenge's resource constraints. EUMU contains 239.169M parameters, requires 23.947 GFLOPs, uses 4.5 GB of peak inference memory, and achieves a final challenge score of 17.3409. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Dayoung-Kil/EUMU.

</details>


### [11] [ParticleSplat: Self-supervised Object-centric Latent Particle Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19463)
*Lyuxing He,Daniel Guo,Elizabeth Terveen,Deepak Pathak,David Held,Tal Daniel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ParticleSplat，一种基于3D高斯泼溅的自监督物体中心学习方法，将场景分解为潜在“粒子”，解决传统2D方法无法进行3D几何推理的问题，支持无监督物体掩码学习和机器人操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Deep Latent Particles (DLP)框架仅具有2D性质，缺乏明确的3D空间和几何推理能力，限制了其在机器人操作等下游任务中的应用。

Method: 利用潜在粒子与3D高斯原语的结构相似性，构建3D潜在粒子空间。通过新视角合成目标训练模型，将多视图及相机位姿编码到共享的3D物体中心潜在空间中，并将粒子转换为粒子对齐的3D高斯以重建场景。

Result: 该方法在无监督情况下自动学习物体掩码，支持通过修改潜在空间中的粒子实现可控的3D场景编辑（如移动物体）。在模拟和真实世界数据集上验证了其有效性，并证明其3D表示提升了机器人操作任务的下游性能。

Conclusion: ParticleSplat通过结合3D高斯泼溅与物体中心学习，实现了有效的3D场景分解与理解，为需要几何感知的下游任务提供了更优的表征基础。

Abstract: We present ParticleSplat, a self-supervised object-centric learning method that decomposes scenes into a set of latent ''particles'' representing semantic entities through feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting. Building on the Deep Latent Particles (DLP) framework, which represents images as a set of particles with attributes such as position, scale, and visual appearance, we address a key limitation of DLP: its inherently 2D nature, which prevents explicit 3D spatial and geometric reasoning that are critical for downstream tasks such as robotic manipulation. Leveraging the structural similarity between latent particles and 3D Gaussian primitives, we introduce a 3D latent particle space trained with a novel view synthesis objective. Our model jointly encodes multiple views with camera poses into a shared 3D object-centric latent space, then transforms particles into particle-aligned 3D Gaussians whose composition reconstructs the full scene. On simulated and real-world datasets, we show that this formulation inherently learns object masks without supervision and supports controllable 3D scene editing, such as moving objects by modifying particles in the latent space. We further establish that the learned 3D representation improves downstream performance on robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [12] [SCOUT: Sim-to-Real Text-Based Person Retrieval by Embedding-Space Prediction over Frozen Video Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19483)
*Abdarahmane Traoré,Andy Couturier,Éric Hervet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCOUT框架，利用冻结的视频和文本编码器及可训练预测器解决跨模态检索问题，在AI City Challenge 2026中取得84.25 mAP@10的高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决基于文本的人员检索中合成数据到真实数据的差距（sim-to-real gap）问题，通常需使用昂贵的微调交叉编码器；本研究探索冻结编码器系统能否与之竞争。

Method: 将跨模态检索建模为嵌入空间中的预测任务。使用冻结的视频编码器（V-JEPA）和文本编码器（EmbeddingGemma），通过从Qwen3.5-0.8B初始化的可训练预测器，在双向InfoNCE目标下映射补丁标记。结合参数高效的ExPLoRA适配和基于视觉语言模型的免训练属性分解重排序器。

Result: 1. 最佳冻结文本编码器取决于其几何结构与视频特征的匹配度，训练-free对齐评分能准确预测检索精度。2. 引入ExPLoRA适配和重排序器使排行榜R@1提升2.2分。3. 完整系统在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4达到84.25 mAP@10，而单模型仅为60.63。训练成本约95 GPU小时。

Conclusion: 冻结编码器系统通过适当的预测器和后处理优化，能在不微调基础编码器的情况下实现高性能跨模态检索，证明了高效对齐和几何匹配的重要性，并为低成本、高效果的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Text-based person retrieval under a sim-to-real gap (synthetic training data, a real-image gallery) is usually tackled with costly fine-tuned cross-encoders. We ask whether a frozen-encoder system can compete. We present SCOUT, which casts cross-modal retrieval as prediction in embedding space. A trainable predictor maps the patch tokens of a frozen video encoder into the embedding space of a frozen text encoder under a bidirectional InfoNCE objective, and no encoder is fine-tuned in the base model. The video encoder is V-JEPA, the text encoder is EmbeddingGemma, and the predictor is initialized from a Qwen3.5-0.8B decoder. We make three findings. First, the best frozen text encoder is simply the one whose geometry best matches the video features. A training-free alignment score ranks three candidate text encoders in the same order as their retrieval accuracy on our held-out split (Spearman $ρ= 1.0$); a fourth, LLM-based encoder shows the rule is metric-dependent, holding for a neighborhood-overlap score ($ρ= 0.8$) but not for a linear probe ($ρ= -0.2$). Second, two precision-targeted levers, parameter-efficient ExPLoRA adaptation of the video encoder and a training-free attribute-decomposed reranker built on a vision-language model, improve the top-rank precision that otherwise limits the frozen system, adding 2.2 points of leaderboard R@1. Third, a local-versus-public calibration study explains which interventions transfer to the real domain. On AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4 the full retrieve-fuse-rerank system reaches 84.25 mAP@10 on the final leaderboard, while a single frozen model submitted alone reaches 60.63. Our trained components cost about 95 GPU-hours. CMP, the dataset authors' fine-tuned cross-encoder that trains for sixteen GPU-days, is one fusion member of the full system, not an alternative. Code and annotations: https://github.com/abtraore/SCOUT-ECCV

</details>


### [13] [AMB3R-SLAM: Kilometer-scale SLAM with Hierarchical Backend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19518)
*Hengyi Wang,Lourdes Agapito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AMB3R-SLAM是一种实时单目SLAM系统，能在消费级GPU上重建公里级轨迹。它通过轻量前端和分层后端实现高效跟踪与一致性约束，无需依赖静态世界假设的束调整即可处理动态场景，并支持多模态输入（立体、RGB-D、LiDAR），在多个数据集上将绝对轨迹误差（ATE）显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM方法常受限于对静态世界的假设，难以有效处理复杂动态场景，且在高精度与实时性之间难以平衡，尤其是在长距离轨迹重建中。

Method: 提出AMB3R-SLAM架构，结合轻量级前端进行低延迟在线跟踪，以及分层后端以渐进方式施加局部、中级和全局一致性约束；摒弃基于静态世界假设的束调整算法，并扩展支持立体视觉、RGB-D和LiDAR等多源输入。

Result: 在9个数据集上验证了强大的相机跟踪性能，在VBR和Oxford Spires数据集上将绝对轨迹误差（ATE）较此前最先进方法降低超过70%；结合LiDAR输入后，在KITTI和VBR数据集上进一步将ATE降至亚米级。

Conclusion: AMB3R-SLAM通过解耦动态场景处理与多模态融合能力，实现了高精度、实时且可扩展的单目SLAM解决方案，为大规模动态环境下的三维重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present AMB3R-SLAM, a real-time monocular SLAM system capable of reconstructing kilometer-scale trajectories over 10k frames on a single consumer-grade GPU. Our model couples a lightweight front-end for low-latency online tracking with a hierarchical backend that progressively enforces local, mid-level, and global consistency. By avoiding bundle adjustment that relies on the static world assumption, our system naturally handles complex dynamic scenes out of the box. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our method can be extended to leverage stereo, RGB-D, and LiDAR as additional inputs. AMB3R-SLAM achieves strong camera tracking performance across 9 datasets, reducing the absolute trajectory error (ATE) of previous state-of-the-art methods on VBR and Oxford Spires by over 70%. With additional LiDAR input, our model further reduces ATE to sub-meter level on KITTI and VBR datasets.

</details>


### [14] [PerSeM: Persistent Semantic Memory for Long-Horizon Open-Vocabulary UAV Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19542)
*Saurbh Singh Jamwal,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PerSeM框架，通过构建基于多数投票的持久化3D语义记忆并辅以保守的空间细化策略，解决无人机在长时序开放词汇分割中的时空不一致问题，无需重新训练即可显著提升语义准确性和时间稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放词汇分割方法仅关注帧级预测，导致无人机在重复观测和视角变化时出现时间上不一致的问题，难以支持长时序的语义映射任务。

Method: 提出PerSeM（Persistent Semantic Memory），这是一种无需训练的持久化语义记忆框架。该方法将帧级语义观测与持久化的世界空间体素关联，构建基于多数投票的语义记忆，并通过历史保留的空间细化、信任感知的重放以及上下文引导的验证进行保守式的精炼。

Result: 在Forest和UAVScenes基准测试中，持久化3D记忆相比帧级预测在语义正确性和时间稳定性上获得了显著增益。在所有评估的UAVScenes序列中均实现了持续的性能提升，且改进主要集中在语义困难和时间不稳定的区域。

Conclusion: 持久化3D聚合为长时序语义映射提供了坚实基础，而对不确定记忆状态的保守式精炼可以在不依赖重新训练或额外神经网络推理的情况下提供额外的性能提升。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary segmentation enables rich semantic perception for UAVs, but frame-wise predictions can remain temporally inconsistent across repeated observations and changing viewpoints. We present PerSeM, a training-free persistent semantic memory framework for long-horizon open-vocabulary UAV mapping. PerSeM associates frame-wise semantic observations with persistent world-space voxels and constructs a majority-based semantic memory, which is conservatively refined through history-preserving spatial refinement, trust-aware replay, and context-guided verification. Experiments on the Forest and UAVScenes benchmarks show that persistent 3D memory provides substantial gains in semantic correctness and temporal stability over frame-wise predictions. Beyond this strong persistent-memory baseline, PerSeM provides consistent additional improvements, improving both semantic accuracy and temporal stability across all five evaluated UAVScenes sequences. Analysis using regions identified independently of the final PerSeM predictions further shows that these gains are concentrated in semantically difficult and temporally unstable regions, where majority-based memory is most likely to remain uncertain. These results demonstrate that persistent 3D aggregation provides a strong foundation for long-horizon semantic mapping, while conservative refinement of uncertain memory states can provide additional improvements without retraining or additional neural-network inference.

</details>


### [15] [A Multi-Modal Generative Model for Tomato Disease Leaves Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19555)
*Khang Nguyen Quoc,Minh-Phuoc Tran,Gia-Han Truong,Luyl-Da Quach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SOLAR，一种用于番茄病害分析的多模态生成式VQA模型。通过融合专家模块对齐视觉与语言特征，实现症状识别、严重程度评估及问答推理的统一框架，在41,677张图像和21.6万问答对上显著优于现有单模态及特定任务模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有植物病害分析多局限于孤立预测任务，缺乏对症状识别、严重程度评估与诊断推理间互补关系的捕捉，难以满足精准农业中综合、可解释的病害理解需求。

Method: 构建SOLAR多模态生成模型，将番茄病害分析形式化为生成式视觉问答（VQA）任务。引入基于混合专家（MoE）的Fusion Expert模块，实现视觉特征与任务感知语言表示的对齐，支持跨六种问答任务的上下文相关答案生成。

Result: 在包含41,677张图像和216,209个问答对的数据集上，SOLAR在封闭和开放端问答设置中均表现出 superior 准确性、鲁棒性及多模态推理能力，全面超越当前最先进的纯视觉、视觉-语言及特定任务模型。

Conclusion: 生成式多模态建模为植物病害理解提供了有效方向，通过统一框架实现多任务推理与知识共享，提升了病害分析的灵活性与性能。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence for plant disease analysis has advanced from task-specific classifiers to multi-modal models capable of jointly interpreting visual and textual information. However, practical deployment in precision agriculture remains limited because most existing approaches treat disease understanding as isolated prediction tasks, failing to capture the complementary relationships among symptom recognition, severity assessment, and question-driven diagnostic reasoning. In tomato pathology, accurate interpretation of diseased leaves requires more than label prediction; it demands integrating visual symptoms with semantic context to support a comprehensive and explainable understanding. Here, we present SOLAR, a multimodal generative model that understands tomato disease spanning six question-answering tasks. SOLAR learns to align visual features with task-aware language representations by Fusion Expert module based on mixture-of-expert, enabling it to generate contextually relevant answers across diverse diagnostic tasks. By formulating tomato disease analysis as a generative Visual Question Answering (VQA) task, SOLAR provides a flexible framework that supports multi-task inference within a single model while improving performance and cross-task knowledge sharing. We evaluate SOLAR on $41,677$ images, including $216,209$ Question-Answering (QA) pairs to understand tomato leaf disease under both closed and open-ended QA settings. Experimental results show that SOLAR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art vision-only, vision-language, and task-specific models across all tasks, demonstrating superior accuracy, robustness, and multimodal reasoning. These findings highlight the potential of generative multimodal modeling as an effective direction for understanding of plant disease. The code for this study is available at https://github.com/EnalisUs/SOLAR.

</details>


### [16] [Selective Cotton Boll Localization for Robotic Harvesting: Evaluation of Deep Learning Vision Models Under Field Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19592)
*Thevathayarajh Thayananthan,Xin Zhang,Isuru Laddusinghe Badu,Jonathan Harjono,Glen C. Rains,Beiwen Li,Leonardo M. Bastos,Nuwan K. Wijewardane,Vitor S. Martins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了一种基于深度学习的选择性机器人采棉感知框架。通过对比YOLO系列检测模型与多种分割模型，发现YOLOv12-m-seg在精度与速度平衡上表现最佳（AP@0.5为83.7%，推理时间20.4ms），并在UR5e机器人实地实验中验证了其实时检测、分割及选择性采摘的有效性，证明其具备田间部署潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对选择性机器人棉花采摘任务，需要开发高效、准确的视觉感知框架，以解决复杂自然光照和天气条件下棉铃的实时检测与精确分割问题，从而支持自动化采摘作业。

Method: 收集了1,008张包含不同光照和天气条件的田间图像作为数据集。评估了从YOLOv8到YOLOv13的检测模型默认配置；评估了YOLOv8/11/12-seg、SAM、SAMv2.1、FastSAM及Grounded-SAM+RAM等分割模型的性能。此外，还测试了由GELAN-s生成边界框提示的检测引导分割方法，并使用UR5e机械臂、定制末端执行器和ZED2i立体相机进行实地实验验证。

Result: 在检测模型中，GELAN-s实现了精度与速度的最佳平衡（mAP 86.1%，推理时间42.3ms）。在直接分割模型中，YOLOv12-m-seg在AP@0.5（83.7%）和FPS之间取得最优平衡，推理时间为20.4ms。检测引导分割中，SAMv2.1 Tiny优于FastSAM和Grounded-SAM。面积评估显示，YOLOv12-m-seg的R²值为0.966，显著高于GELAN-s + SAMv2.1 Tiny的0.860。实地实验证实了YOLOv12-m-seg在不同置信度下实现实时检测和选择性采摘的有效性。

Conclusion: YOLOv12-m-seg提供了一种高效的感知模型，适用于机器人棉花收获，具有强大的田间部署潜力，能够为选择性采摘提供可靠的实时检测、分割和定位支持。

Abstract: This study developed and evaluated a deep-learning-based perception framework for selective robotic cotton picking. The dataset contained 1,008 annotated field images collected using three cameras under varying natural lighting and weather conditions. Object-detection models from the YOLOv8 through YOLOv13 families were evaluated using their default configurations, while segmentation performance was assessed using YOLOv8-seg, YOLOv11-seg, YOLOv12-seg, the Segment Anything Model (SAM), SAMv2.1, FastSAM, and Grounded-SAM with the Recognize Anything Model (RAM). Among the detection models, GELAN-s achieved the most favorable balance between mean average precision (mAP) and inference speed, obtaining an mAP of 86.1%, precision of 81.6%, recall of 76.6%, and an F1-score of 79.0%, with an average inference time of 42.3 ms per image. Among the direct segmentation models, YOLOv12-m-seg provided the most favorable balance between AP@0.5 and FPS, achieving a segmentation AP@0.5 of 83.7% with an inference time of 20.4 ms per image. In the detection-prompted segmentation approach, bounding-box prompts generated by GELAN-s improved the localization of cotton bolls for SAM and SAMv2.1, while SAMv2.1 Tiny consistently outperformed FastSAM and Grounded-SAM with RAM. In the area-based evaluation against manually annotated segmentation masks, YOLOv12-m-seg achieved an $R^2$ value of 0.966, compared with 0.860 for GELAN-s + SAMv2.1 Tiny. Field experiments conducted using a UR5e robotic manipulator, a custom end-effector, and a ZED2i stereo camera further validated the effectiveness of the YOLOv12-m-seg model for real-time cotton boll detection, segmentation, and selective picking under varying confidence levels. These results demonstrate that YOLOv12-m-seg provides an efficient perception model for robotic cotton harvesting and has strong potential for field deployment.

</details>


### [17] [VGGT-GS SLAM: Uncalibrated Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM with Feed-Forward Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19628)
*Yuhang Han,Hao Wang,Jiaxi Cao,Xingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VGGT-GS SLAM，一种基于前馈先验、通过子图可微束调整和高斯原生对齐实现无标定视频单目3D高斯溅射SLAM的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无标定视频下的单目SLAM问题，旨在提升定位精度并保证高质量的渲染效果，建立无标定高斯SLAM的强基线。

Method: 利用VGGT的前馈姿态和深度先验，执行子图可微束调整以联合优化相机姿态和3D高斯地图；通过解析校准雅可比矩阵优化子图共享的内参及径向-切向畸变；引入高斯原生对齐（GNA）进行连续子图间的尺度 refinement 和回环检测验证。

Result: 在标准室内基准测试中，该系统在无标定设置下显著提升了定位准确性，并保持了强大的渲染质量。

Conclusion: VGGT-GS SLAM为无标定环境下的3D高斯溅射SLAM提供了强有力的性能基线和解决方案。

Abstract: We present VGGT-GS SLAM, a monocular 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM system designed for uncalibrated videos. Starting from feed-forward VGGT pose and depth priors, our system performs submap differentiable bundle adjustment that jointly refines camera poses and a 3D Gaussian map, while optimizing submap-shared intrinsics and radial--tangential distortion through analytic calibration Jacobians. To improve global consistency, we introduce Gaussian-native alignment (GNA) for camera-anchored scale refinement between sequential submaps and verification of loop-closure candidates. Extensive experiments on standard indoor benchmarks show consistent improvements in localization accuracy and strong rendering quality under uncalibrated settings, establishing a strong baseline for uncalibrated Gaussian SLAM.

</details>


### [18] [Instance Segmentation and Fine-grained Classification for Urban Buildings with Adaptive Region Dividing and Spatially-Supervised Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19631)
*Weiyuan Zhang,Qi Zhang,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应区域划分策略和统一场景级评估方法，结合空间监督对比损失的细粒度分类模型，解决了大规模点云中建筑实例分割与功能分类的碎片化及类别不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预定义块进行训练和评估，这在真实应用中不可用，且会引入额外预处理并破坏建筑结构的完整性；同时，缺乏对城市建筑细粒度功能分类的探索。

Method: 1. 自适应区域划分：将3D点云投影到鸟瞰图(BEV)，利用预训练分割模型检测建筑区域，再将边界框反投影回原始点云构建结构对齐的自适应训练块。2. 细粒度分类模型：使用Point Transformer编码器融合几何、颜色和核心上下文信息；采用类别平衡加权交叉熵处理类别不平衡；引入空间监督对比损失以增强类间判别性。

Result: 在UrbanBIS和STPLS3D数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在建筑实例分割和细粒度分类方面优于现有的最先进(SOTA)方法。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应划分策略和细粒度分类模型有效提升了大规模城市点云处理的准确性和实用性，为数字城市建模提供了更可靠的技术支持。

Abstract: Accurate instance-level and functional understanding of urban buildings in large-scale point clouds is essential for digital city modeling and urban analysis. However, the extensive spatial coverage of urban scenes leads most existing methods to rely on predefined blocks for training and evaluation, although such partitions are rarely available in real-world applications and introduce additional preprocessing while fragmenting complete building structures. To address this issue, we propose an adaptive region-dividing strategy with unified scene-level evaluation. Specifically, the 3D point cloud is projected onto a bird's-eye-view (BEV) plane, where a pretrained segmentation model is used to detect building regions. The detected bounding boxes are then back-projected to the original point cloud to construct structure-aligned adaptive training blocks, enabling semantically guided dynamic partitioning without manual design. Furthermore, beyond instance-level understanding, few methods have explored fine-grained classification for urban buildings, and thus we also put forward a fine-grained classification model for urban buildings with a spatially-supervised contrastive loss. First, for each segmented building, a point transformer classifier jointly encodes its body and local context using geometric, color, and core-context information. Then, the class-balanced weighted cross-entropy is used to alleviate severe class imbalance. The proposed spatially-supervised contrastive loss further enhances inter-class discriminability by assigning greater weight to spatially proximate, same-category buildings, encouraging compact functional representations while separating easily confused categories. Extensive experiments on UrbanBIS and STPLS3D demonstrate the advantages of the proposed method in building instance segmentation and fine-grained classification compared to existing SOTA methods.

</details>


### [19] [Scientific Image Quality Assessment via Multi-modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19634)
*Yinuo Zhang,Bingshuo Liu,Zhiying Tu,Dianhui Chu,Qingbin Liu,Xi Chen,Jiang Bian,Xiaoyan Yu,Dianbo Sui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于科学图像质量评估（SIQA）的检索增强生成（RAG）框架，通过构建多模态索引和多路由检索融合机制，同时解决理解（SIQA-U）和评分（SIQA-S）任务。该方法在ICME 2026 SIQA挑战赛的SIQA-U赛道中荣获第一名，且评估结果与人类专家判断高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决科学图像质量评估中复杂图像的评估难题，特别是需要同时兼顾语义理解（SIQA-U）和数值评分（SIQA-S）的双重需求，以提升大语言模型对科学图像质量的评估能力。

Method: 1. 构建多模态索引：整合文本语义与细粒度视觉特征；
2. 开发多路由检索与融合机制：为大语言模型提供高相关性的参考案例；
3. 利用检索增强生成（RAG）框架增强模型对复杂科学图像的理解与评估能力。

Result: 实验表明该框架有效对齐了人类专家的评判标准。在ICME 2026 Grand Challenges的SIQA挑战赛SIQA-U赛道中取得第1名的优异成绩。

Conclusion: 提出的RAG框架通过结合多模态检索与大语言模型，显著提升了科学图像质量评估的性能，特别是在理解任务上达到了顶尖水平，证明了该方法在自动化科学图像评估中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework for scientific image quality assessment, designed to simultaneously address both the understanding track (SIQA-U) and the scoring track (SIQA-S) of the SIQA challenge. We construct a multimodal index that integrates textual semantics with fine-grained visual features, and develop a multi-route retrieval and fusion mechanism to provide large language models with highly relevant reference cases, thereby enhancing their capability to evaluate complex scientific images. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively aligns with the judgment criteria of human experts. Ultimately, our method achieves 1st place in the SIQA-U track of the SIQA challenge at the ICME 2026 Grand Challenges.

</details>


### [20] [Towards Active Cross-View Object Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19662)
*Shunyu Yao,Xiaohan Zhang,Zhuoran Yang,Haoqi Lai,Qi Ming,Xiaoxi Hu,Hui-Liang Shen,Si-Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ActiveGeo框架及ActiveMoPT方法，通过主动选择视角和停止策略优化跨视图物体地理定位，在MoP-UAV和ActiveGeo-858数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统跨视图物体地理定位（CVOGL）假设查询图像固定，忽略了移动代理主动获取更多信息观察的能力。

Method: 提出ActiveMoPT框架，包含三阶段训练：1) 多视图提示保留适应以聚合多视图并复用初始提示；2) 轨迹引导的策略初始化利用监督轨迹学习视角选择和初始停止行为；3) 成本感知策略细化使用GRPO结合收益-成本奖励联合优化定位精度和观测效率。同时构建ActiveGeo-858零样本测试集。

Result: 在MoP-UAV上平均仅需1.45个查询视图即达到SOTA性能；在ActiveGeo-858零样本评估中显著优于先前的CVOGL方法。

Conclusion: 主动式跨视图物体地理定位能有效提升定位精度与观测效率，所提方法在多种基准上表现优异。

Abstract: Cross-view object geo-localization (CVOGL) typically assumes a fixed query image, overlooking the ability of mobile agents to actively acquire more informative observations. To address this limitation, we introduce Active Cross-View Object Geo-Localization (ActiveGeo), where an agent sequentially selects new viewpoints and determines when to stop, aiming to improve localization with minimal observations. We further propose ActiveMoPT, an ActiveGeo framework with three-stage training. First, Multi-View Prompt-Preserving Adaptation enables the model to aggregate multiple query views while reusing the initial prompt. Second, Trajectory-Guided Policy Initialization uses supervised agent trajectories to learn viewpoint selection and initial stopping behavior. Third, Cost-Aware Policy Refinement employs GRPO with a gain-cost reward to jointly optimize localization accuracy and observation efficiency. We also construct ActiveGeo-858, a zero-shot test set containing 858 scenes and 1,716 target annotations. Experiments show that ActiveMoPT achieves state-of-the-art performance on MoP-UAV using only 1.45 query views on average, and substantially outperforms previous CVOGL approaches under zero-shot evaluation on ActiveGeo-858.

</details>


### [21] [VideoResearcher: Self-Improving Tool Design for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19664)
*Dingqiang Ye,Dongdi Zhao,Kaishen Wang,Qingqiao Hu,Jingchen Sun,Yijun Liang,Yuqi Jia,Yiqiao Huang,Yunjie Tian,Jiaxing Zhang,Chuanyang Jin,Ke Zhang,Vishal M. Patel,Di Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoResearcher，一种无需训练的multi-agent框架，通过自主设计、测试和精炼视频工具来解决长视频理解中手动工程成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频智能体系统依赖昂贵且耗时的人工设计与试错；现有的自我改进方法存在局限，如仅优化低影响力提示、重组预定义微工具或在Harness优化中难以收敛。

Method: 构建VideoResearcher框架，包含“解决（Solving）”和“进化（Evolving）”双循环机制：分析工具使用轨迹以识别能力缺口，协调专用智能体开发并验证可执行工具，复用进化后的工具增强后续视频推理中的证据获取，全程无需更新模型参数。

Result: 在自我改进型智能体中达到最先进性能，接近人工设计的上限，证明了无需训练范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自主工具开发扩展了智能体能力，同时降低了高昂的人工工程成本，为长视频理解提供了一种新的无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Video agents have made substantial progress in long-video understanding. Yet effective video-agent systems require costly, time-consuming manual design and trial and error. Current self-improvement methods either refine low-impact prompts, recombine predefined micro-tools, or struggle with convergence in harness optimization. To bridge this gap, we target high-impact video-tool with VideoResearcher, a training-free multi-agent framework that autonomously designs, tests, and refines tools for video understanding, like a human researcher. VideoResearcher operates through dual Solving and Evolving loops: it analyzes tool-use trajectories to identify capability gaps, coordinates specialized agents to develop and validate executable tools, and reuses evolved tools to strengthen evidence acquisition in subsequent video reasoning. Through iterative tool refinement and validation, it progressively strengthens evidence acquisition without updating model parameters. VideoResearcher achieves state-of-the-art performance among self-improving agents and approaches the human-designed upper bound, demonstrating a training-free paradigm for long-video understanding that expands agent capabilities through autonomous tool development while reducing costly manual engineering.

</details>


### [22] [Beyond Patch Removal: Persistent Adversarial Effects in Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19669)
*Enhao Wu,Fusen Guo,Yuxin Cao,Ziyang Lyu,Lin Li,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究揭示了针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的对抗性补丁不仅导致即时动作损坏，还会产生移除后仍存在的持久状态效应。通过引入状态恢复协议和控制实验，证实了这些持久效应的存在，并表明及时干预对于系统恢复至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注连续攻击，未能区分对抗性攻击导致的即时动作损坏与移除补丁后残留的持久状态效应，缺乏对后者及其可恢复性的系统性分析。

Method: 1. 引入状态恢复协议：在匹配的动作块边界处移除补丁，并在相同的剩余步骤预算下测量后续的可恢复性。
2. 设置对照组：包括清洁、随机补丁、偏差匹配和固定方向控制，以区分对抗性效应与遮挡、动作误差幅度和方向持续性。
3. 评估恢复适配器：在受控干预延迟下，训练基于攻击诱导状态的恢复适配器。
4. 在OpenVLA-OFT（使用EDPA攻击）和自回归OpenVLA上进行评估。

Result: 1. 在LIBERO-Long任务中，经过五个动作块后，仅36.2%的片段可恢复，而偏差匹配和固定方向对照组分别为89.9%和87.0%，证明对抗效应具有持久性。
2. 自回归OpenVLA也观察到类似的持久效应。
3. 恢复适配器将一阶延迟下的恢复率从7.7%提升至47.4%，但随着干预延迟增加，其效果显著下降。

Conclusion: 对抗性效应可以在补丁移除后持续存在，且及时的干预是实现系统恢复的关键因素。

Abstract: Adversarial patches to Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies can cause both immediate action corruption and persistent state effects that remain after the patch is removed. Existing evaluations largely focus on continuous attacks and do not separate these two effects. We introduce a state-restoration protocol that removes the patch at matched action-chunk boundaries and measures subsequent recoverability under the same remaining step budget. Clean, random-patch, deviation-matched, and fixed-direction controls distinguish adversarial effects from occlusion, action-error magnitude, and directional persistence. We also evaluate a recovery adapter trained on attack-induced states under controlled intervention latency. On OpenVLA-OFT with EDPA attacks, only 36.2% of LIBERO-Long episodes remain recoverable after five chunks, compared with 89.9% and 87.0% for the deviation-matched and fixed-direction controls. Similar persistent effects are observed on autoregressive OpenVLA. The recovery adapter improves recovery from 7.7% to 47.4% at one-chunk latency, but its benefit decreases substantially with delayed intervention. These results show that adversarial effects can persist after patch removal and that timely intervention is critical for recovery.

</details>


### [23] [IMFD: End-to-end Multi-Face Forgery Detection through Instruction-based Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19693)
*Dasom Choi,Sangjun Moon,Hyeongchan Im,Jaeeon Park,Jingun Kwon,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe,Manabu Okumura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IMFD，一种基于指令的大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的单阶段多脸伪造检测器，通过将预测的面部边界框作为视觉提示融入指令，实现端到端的面部定位与伪造标签预测，显著提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多脸伪造检测器独立裁剪和验证每张人脸，忽略了背景上下文和人脸间的相互关系，导致性能不佳；同时需要一种能理解整张图像并遵循复杂文本指令的方法。

Method: 构建IMFD（Instruction-based Multi-face Forgery Detector），利用指令式大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）进行端到端训练，联合执行面部定位和单脸伪造标签预测；创新性地将被预测的面部边界框显式整合为指令中的视觉线索，以增强指令接地能力和伪造检测效果；并将现有的多脸伪造数据集转换为指令格式以支持训练和评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，IMFD通过整合面部边界框到指令中，有效提升了多脸伪造检测性能，并在多项指标上持续优于各种最先进（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: IMFD提供了一种简单有效的单阶段多脸伪造检测框架，证明了将视觉定位信息融入指令式大模型可显著改善对复杂多脸场景的伪造检测能力。

Abstract: The rapid increase of deepfakes has raised significant concerns due to their spread on social media. Traditional multi-face forgery detectors crop and verify each face independently, ignoring background context and inter-face relationships, which often yields suboptimal performance. To overcome these limitations, we leverage instruction-based Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), which can interpret entire images and follow complex textual instructions. We propose a simple yet effective single-stage multi-face forgery detector, called IMFD (Instruction-based Multi-face Forgery Detector), which is trained end-to-end to jointly localize faces and predict per-face forgery labels. Rather than treating face box prediction only as a joint objective, IMFD explicitly integrates predicted face bounding boxes into the instruction as visual cues that enhance instruction grounding and forgery detection. To support the training and evaluation of IMFD, we convert existing multi-face forgery datasets into an instruction-based format. Experimental results and analyses show that IMFD improves multi-face forgery detection by integrating face bounding boxes into the instruction, and consistently outperforms various state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [24] [Understanding and Exploiting Diagonal Attention Sparsity in Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19702)
*Daeun Kim,Junwha Hong,Changhun Oh,Yoonsung Kim,Yoonhyeong Lee,Jongse Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对自回归图像生成中KV缓存访问瓶颈，提出对角线感知稀疏注意力机制，在保持高质量的同时显著提升吞吐量（3.1x）并降低延迟（1.19x）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自回归图像生成因逐token解码导致的KV缓存访问瓶颈，利用视觉应用对质量轻微下降的容忍度，探索稀疏注意力在该场景的有效性。

Method: 系统分析自回归图像生成的注意力稀疏性特征，发现预填充-解码不对称性及对角线稀疏模式；据此提出对角线感知稀疏注意力机制，选择性跳过窗口内对角线方向的KV条目，基于FlexGen等GPU服务系统实现。

Result: 相比密集推理，该方法实现最高3.1倍的吞吐量提升和1.19倍的延迟改善，且质量退化低于2%。

Conclusion: 自回归图像生成的注意力稀疏性具有独特规律（如对角线模式），专用稀疏机制能有效平衡性能与质量，为多模态AI系统优化提供新路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive image generation has emerged as a paradigm for multimodal AI systems due to its compatibility with transformer-based LLM serving infrastructures. However, generating thousands of visual tokens per request makes decoding increasingly bottlenecked by KV cache accesses during attention computation. Sparse attention is particularly attractive for this workload because many visual generation applications tolerate moderate quality degradation in exchange for improved performance and efficiency. While sparse attention has been extensively explored for text-based LLM inference, it remains unclear whether its sparsity assumptions generalize effectively to autoregressive image generation. We present the first systematic characterization of attention sparsity in autoregressive image generation across diverse workloads and representative open-source models. Our analysis reveals several distinguishing properties, including a pronounced prefill-decode asymmetry, strong attention concentration on prompt and local tokens, and a unique diagonal attention sparsity pattern arising from the spatial locality of visual tokens. Motivated by these observations, we propose a diagonal-aware sparse attention mechanism that selectively skips KV entries along the diagonal attention direction within a recent window. Implemented on top of a GPU-based serving system using FlexGen, FlashAttention-2, and custom kernels, our approach achieves up to 3.1x throughput and 1.19x latency improvements with less than 2% quality degradation compared to dense inference.

</details>


### [25] [GAPrompt++: Multi-Granular Geometry-Aware Point Cloud Prompt for 3D Vision Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19716)
*Zixiang Ai,Zhenyu Cui,Yufei Guo,Wenwen Qiang,Lei Chen,Jiwen Lu,Jiahuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GAPrompt++，一种多粒度几何感知提示方法，用于3D点云的高效参数微调（PEFT），在保持极低可训练参数比例（<2%）的同时，性能超越全量微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的PEFT方法忽视了点云的内在几何结构，无法有效编码细粒度几何线索和粗粒度结构语义，导致适应能力受限；同时全量微调计算成本高且存储占用大。

Method: 1. Point Shift Prompter：提取多尺度多粒度几何特征，实现实例特定的几何调整；2. Keypoint Prompter：自适应生成点级提示以突出局部几何显著性和细粒度结构细节；3. Prompt Propagation机制：将多粒度几何线索注入特征提取层级，强化关键几何特征的捕捉能力。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，GAPrompt++达到了基于提示的PEFT方法的最先进（SOTA）性能，甚至超越了全量微调，且仅需不到2%的可训练参数。此外，构建了基于3D高斯泼溅和多视图立体重建的新基准数据集。

Conclusion: GAPrompt++通过引入多粒度几何感知提示机制，有效解决了现有PEFT方法在几何信息利用上的不足，实现了高效且高性能的3D任务适配，并为未来研究提供了更具挑战性的评估基准。

Abstract: Pre-trained 3D vision models have substantially advanced point cloud analysis, yet adapting them to downstream tasks via full fine-tuning is computationally expensive and storage-intensive. Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) offers a promising alternative by reducing both adaptation cost and storage burden. However, existing prompting-based approaches ignore the intrinsic geometric structures of point clouds, thereby limiting their adaptation capability. This limitation stems from their inability to encode both fine-grained geometric cues and coarse-grained structural semantics, as well as failing to propagate such information effectively through the model hierarchy. To address these challenges, we propose GAPrompt++, a multi-granular geometry-aware prompting method that provides richer geometric guidance for efficient 3D task adaptation. Specifically, we introduce a Point Shift Prompter that extracts multi-granular geometric features across different scales, enabling instance-specific geometric adjustments during adaptation. Next, a Keypoint Prompter adaptively generates point-level prompts to highlight local geometric saliency and fine-grained structural details. Furthermore, a Prompt Propagation mechanism injects these multi-granular geometric cues throughout the feature extraction hierarchy, strengthening the ability to capture essential geometric characteristics. Extensive experiments show that GAPrompt++ achieves state-of-the-art performance among prompting-based PEFT methods and even surpasses full fine-tuning across diverse benchmarks, while requiring less than 2\% trainable parameters. In addition, to address the saturation of existing evaluation datasets, we construct two more challenging benchmarks derived from 3D Gaussian Splatting and Multi-View Stereo reconstruction, offering diverse and realistic point cloud scenarios to promote future research.

</details>


### [26] [SeetaPsych v1.0: An Open-source Computer Vision Toolkit for Behavior-based Psychological Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19719)
*Jiabei Zeng,Chiqin Li,Kaizhou Li,Fei Chang,Yong Li,Yuanhao Zhao,Dan Han,Wenqiang Yang,Xilin Chen,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeetaPsych v1.0 是一个开源、统一且可扩展的计算机视觉工具包，旨在从面部图像和视频中提取心理相关信号，包含情绪分析、心率估计、注视点估计等核心模块，支持模块化流水线架构和标准化 API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的行为导向心理测量技术模块通常分散在特定任务系统中，具有异构接口和不同的部署要求，缺乏统一和可扩展的平台。

Method: 开发 SeetaPsych v1.0，集成四大核心模块（基于面部的情绪分析、基于摄像头的估计、屏幕注视点估计、场景跟随注视）及辅助预处理模块（人脸检测、关键点检测、头部检测），采用模块化 Pipeline/Runner 架构自动解决属性依赖并构建计算图，提供 Python API 和交互式 WebUI。

Result: 成功构建了一个集成的视觉测量平台，实现了中间结果共享、自动化计算图构建以及代码无关的快速方法评估，为心理学、行为科学和人机交互等领域提供了标准化的大规模分析能力。

Conclusion: SeetaPsych 提供了一个整合且易于访问的视觉测量平台，有效解决了现有技术的碎片化问题，促进了基于行为的心理测量的可重复性和大规模应用。

Abstract: Automated visual analysis opens new avenues for behavior--based psychological measurement. Nevertheless, existing technological modules are typically scattered across task specific systems with heterogeneous interfaces and disparate deployment requirements. In this work, we present SeetaPsych v1.0, an open source, unified and extensible computer vision toolkit designed to extract psychologically relevant signals from facial images and/or face based videos. The current release encompasses four major core modules aiming at behavior--based physiological perception: unified face based emotion analysis (simultaneous facial expression recognition, facial action unit detection, and valence--arousal estimation), camera based heart rate estimation, screen point--of--gaze estimation, and scene gaze following. A suite of auxiliary preprocessing modules for human centric visual analysis is also included, comprising face detection, facial landmark detection, and head detection. These functionalities are encapsulated within a modular Pipeline/Runner architecture that automatically resolves attribute dependencies, constructs computation graphs, and support intermediate result sharing among modules. SeetaPsych provides standardized Python APIs to facilitate reproducible, large scale analyses, alongside an interactive WebUI for rapid, code--free method evaluation. Overall, SeetaPsych offers an integrated and accessible visual measurement platform for research in psychology, behavioral science, human computer interaction, and related fields.

</details>


### [27] [Recency Forcing: Bridging the Long-Horizon Gap in Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19729)
*Tri Cao,Hung Nguyen,Phong Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Recency Forcing方法，通过引入Temporal Response Bias (TRB)解决自回归视频生成中的KV缓存驱逐不匹配问题，在零额外推理成本下显著提升长视界生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自回归视频生成中因训练与推理阶段KV缓存状态不一致（即KV eviction mismatch）导致的性能退化问题。传统截断上下文会丢失时间信息并降低运动连贯性。

Method: 1. 定义位置响应 $R( Δ, \, t_{\text{denoise}})$ 分析上下文影响随时间距离和去噪步骤的变化；2. 提出Recency Forcing，应用基于$R$的非正、时间步相关的Temporal Response Bias (TRB)；3. 引入Biased Attention Reparameterization (BAR)，将偏差移至softmax外部，实现零开销的FlashAttention调用。

Result: 在VBench和VBench-Long基准测试中实现了最先进的长视界生成质量，且无需增加额外的推理成本。

Conclusion: 该方法有效弥合了训练-推理差距，既保留了上下文又消除了远处帧被驱逐时的负面影响，支持免训练和基于训练两种模式。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video generation degrades over long horizons due to an overlooked train-inference discrepancy we term KV eviction mismatch: models train on short clips where all context frames reside in the KV cache, but at inference, memory constraints force distant frames to be evicted from the KV cache - removing context the model was conditioned on. Rather than simulating eviction via context truncation - which discards temporal information the model still needs and degrades motion coherence - we keep the context but while progressively reducing the influence of distant frames, making their eventual eviction negligible. To guide this design, we introduce the positional response $R( Δ, \, t_{\text{denoise}})$, a perturbation-based sensitivity measure revealing that context influence decays steeply with temporal distance and varies systematically across denoising steps. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Recency Forcing, which applies a non-positive, timestep-dependent bias, termed Temporal Response Bias (TRB), on pre-softmax attention logits derived directly from $R$, closing the train-inference gap without modifying context length or training objectives. We further introduce Biased Attention Reparameterization (BAR), an exact reformulation that moves the bias outside the softmax, making TRB a standard FlashAttention call at zero overhead. Recency Forcing operates in both training-free mode and training-based mode. Experiments on VBench and VBench-Long demonstrate state-of-the-art long-horizon generation quality at no additional inference cost.

</details>


### [28] [Federated Learning Framework for Privacy-Preserving Kidney Stone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19740)
*Najiyya Younas,Omar Abdulkader,Yaser Ali Shah,Muhammad Jawad Ikram,Jebran Khan,Amaad Khalil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合优化YOLOv8的联邦学习（FL）模型，用于在保护患者隐私的前提下进行CT图像肾结石检测。该模型通过分布式训练避免数据共享，符合GDPR/HIPAA法规，并在边缘设备上实现实时推理，mAP@50达到0.733。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度学习医疗影像诊断中依赖集中式数据存储带来的严重患者隐私泄露和数据安全风险，同时满足GDPR和HIPAA等数据保护法律的合规要求。

Method: 构建耦合优化YOLOv8网络的联邦学习框架。具体包括：1) 采用联邦学习机制，使多家医疗机构在不交换原始患者信息的情况下联合训练通用模型；2) 对YOLOv8进行架构改进，引入残差特征融合（Residual Feature Fusion）和DropBlock正则化技术，以增强检测鲁棒性并减少过拟合。

Result: 在分布式CT数据集上的实验表明，该联邦YOLOv8模型实现了0.733的mAP@50性能指标。系统成功保持了数据机密性，且其轻量化设计支持快速边缘部署和临床环境下的实时推理。

Conclusion: 将联邦学习与先进的目标检测模型相结合，为当代医疗AI辅助诊断提供了一种安全、高效且符合隐私保护要求的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent innovations in deep learning have significantly enhanced the diagnosis of medical images, although they are based on the use of centralized data storage that pose severe threats to patient privacy and medical data security. To address this issue, this research proposes a Federated Learning (FL) model that is coupled with an optimized YOLOv8 network to detect the kidney stones on a computed tomography (CT) image and at the same time, protect privacy of the patients. The suggested system can help various medical organizations to jointly train a common model without exchanging the information about the patients. This is to ensure that data protection laws like GDPR and HIPAA are adhered to. The residual feature fusion and DropBlock regularization among other architectural improvements are also included in YOLOv8 to enhance detection robustness and minimize overfitting. Experimental analysis carried out on a distributed CT dataset demonstrated that the federated YOLOv8 model has a mAP at 50 of 0.733 and is able to keep the data confidential. Moreover, its lean design facilitates fast edge deployment and real-time inference across a clinical setting. Altogether, these findings indicate that Federated Learning is a safe and efficient solution to AI-assisted diagnosis in contemporary healthcare when combined with the use of sophisticated object detection models.

</details>


### [29] [Region-Level Policy Optimization for Fine-grained MLLM Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19745)
*Yuheng Shi,Xiaohuan Pei,Minjing Dong,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Vision-RL2框架，通过解耦定位与识别的分辨率需求，利用强化学习优化轻量级提议网络，以显著减少视觉Token数量并提升多模态大模型（MLLMs）的细粒度视觉感知准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高MLLMs细粒度视觉感知通常依赖增加分辨率，但这导致视觉编码和解码成本激增。研究发现定位（RoI选择）和识别对分辨率的需求不同：定位可容忍更高压缩比。现有端到端解码坐标训练成本高且依赖接地能力，而基于注意力蒸馏的轻量级网络虽快但噪声大，且难以通过答案监督其忠实度。

Method: 提出Vision-RL2方法：1) 从粗略视图进行定位，将高分辨率集中于选定证据；2) 使用从模型注意力中蒸馏出的轻量级提议网络快速生成区域建议；3) 采用区域级强化学习优化提议网络，将连贯区域视为动作，冻结的MLLM读者通过评估区域移除对答案似然的影响来评分；4) 结合互补的减法和加法目标抑制干扰性提议并恢复缺失证据，无需区域标注、响应采样或推理轨迹即可更新预测器；5) 利用精炼后的提议实现稀疏编码，放大证据并排除背景Token。

Result: 在六个细粒度基准测试和四个MLLM骨干网络上，Vision-RL2在所有Token预算下均优于基础模型的准确率，并以约4倍的视觉Token节省量达到了最大预算模型的精度水平。

Conclusion: Vision-RL2通过解耦定位与识别过程并利用强化学习优化区域提议，有效解决了高视觉Token带来的计算成本问题，同时显著提升了MLLMs在细粒度视觉任务上的性能，实现了高效且高精度的视觉感知。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual perception in MLLMs is commonly improved by raising the resolution, but the added visual tokens inflate vision-encoding and language-model prefilling costs. We show that the two operations underlying fine-grained perception, localizing the region of interest (RoI) and recognizing its content, have different resolution requirements. In a controlled diagnostic, localization tolerates roughly 3 to 4 times stronger token compression than recognition, which motivates localizing from a coarse view and concentrating resolution on the selected evidence. Decoding coordinates with the MLLM can be trained end-to-end from answers, but costs a full model pass per query and depends on grounding ability. A lightweight proposal network distilled from the model's attention is fast, but inherits the noise of its attention targets. The RoI from the proposal network reaches the answer through a discrete region choice, so its faithfulness to the answer cannot supervise the network. We therefore optimize the proposal network with region-level reinforcement learning, which we call Vision-RL2. It treats coherent regions as actions, and a frozen MLLM reader scores each one by how its removal changes the answer likelihood. Complementary subtractive and additive objectives suppress distracting proposals and recover missing evidence, updating only the predictor without region annotations, response sampling, or reasoning trajectories. The refined proposal further enables a sparse encoding that magnifies evidence and excludes background tokens. Across six fine-grained benchmarks and four MLLM backbones, Vision-RL2 improves accuracy over the base model at every token budget and surpasses its largest-budget accuracy with about 4 times fewer visual tokens. Code is available at https://github.com/YuHengsss/VisionRL2 .

</details>


### [30] [STAR: Structure-aware Test-time Adaptation for diffusion-based light field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19747)
*Wontae Choi,Ki Ryum Moon,Jae Young Lee,Hyung Sup Yun,Il Yong Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STAR框架，通过测试时适配扩散先验中的三个轻量级适配器，解决从有限噪声焦堆栈重建光场时的空间-角度结构变化问题，优于现有SOTA方法且推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光场重建是高度不适定的逆问题；虽然成像几何固定，但不同场景的光场空间-角度结构（视内细节、视角依赖、视间视差）各异，导致固定的预训练先验无法最优捕捉每个测试光场的结构。

Method: 提出STAR（Structure-aware Test-time Adaptation for diffusion-based light field Reconstruction），冻结预训练扩散先验，为每个测试光场拟合三个轻量级适配器到观测到的焦堆栈数据，以联合适配光场空间-角度结构的三个组成部分。

Result: 在双焦叶和三焦叶设置下，STAR的性能均优于现有最先进方法，且具有比涉及测试时参数更新的方法更短的推理时间。

Conclusion: STAR作为首个针对从焦堆栈重建光场的测试时适配框架，能有效适应不同场景的结构变化，实现高精度快速重建。

Abstract: Light field (LF) reconstruction from limited and noisy focal stack (FS) measurements is a highly ill-posed inverse problem. Although the LF-to-FS imaging geometry is fixed for a given optical setup, LF spatial-angular structure---including within-view spatial details, cross-view angular dependencies, and disparity across views---varies across scenes. Consequently, a fixed pre-trained prior may not optimally capture the spatial-angular structure of each test LF. We propose Structure-aware Test-time Adaptation for diffusion-based light field Reconstruction (STAR), the first test-time adaptation framework for reconstructing an LF from FS. For each test LF, STAR freezes a pre-trained diffusion prior and fits three lightweight adapters to the observed FS to jointly adapt the three components of the LF's spatial-angular structure. STAR outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in both two- and three-focal-sheet settings, with shorter inference times than those with test-time parameter updates.

</details>


### [31] [Benchmarking MLLMs via Cognitive Expected Scene Graph for Safety-Critical Visual Negation Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19767)
*Zhiyun Jiang,Hanyong Wang,Binbin Liang,Yu Xie,Menglong Yang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SNUS任务（基于安全认知的场景否定理解），构建负向描述数据集及CESG评估指标，解决现有MLLM在否定语义下的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型（MLLM）和评估指标存在“肯定偏见”，在处理否定语义时表现不佳；且无约束的视觉否定范式过于开放，缺乏明确边界。

Method: 1. 定义SNUS任务：将否定推理锚定在“安全”这一认知目标上，构建映射局部危险的高保真负向描述数据集。2. 提出CESG Score：一种基于结构 grounding 且感知极性（polarity-aware）的评估指标。

Result: 实验显示现有模型在该任务上表现困难，传统评估指标在语义反转下完全崩溃；提出的框架为SNUS提供了坚实的基准，证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过限定认知目标解决了否定推理的开放性难题，建立了风险感知情境理解和反事实认知的严谨基础。

Abstract: True machine intelligence requires transcending passive pixel registration to master top-down functional reasoning over absent information via visual negation understanding. However, unconstrained visual negation paradigms remain overly open-ended, and pervasive affirmation bias causes both existing Multi-Modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and evaluation metrics to fail under negative semantics. To solve these intertwined challenges systematically, we first anchor the boundaries of negation reasoning within specific cognitive goals. Specifically, by focusing on safety as a highly pragmatic and critical cognitive dimension, we define the task of \textbf{S}cene \textbf{N}egation \textbf{U}nderstanding under \textbf{S}afety Cognition (\textbf{SNUS}). Under this framework, we construct a high-fidelity negative caption dataset mapping dense assertions of localized hazards. Concurrently, we propose the Cognitive Expected Scene Graph (CESG) Score, a structure-grounded, polarity-aware evaluation metric. Extensive experiments demonstrate that while current models struggle on the task, traditional metrics completely collapse under semantic reversals. Conversely, our framework delivers a solid benchmark for SNUS, providing a rigorous foundation to advance risk-aware situational comprehension and counterfactual cognition.

</details>


### [32] [AI Smart Glasses for Wearable Intelligence: From Egocentric Sensing to Agentic Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19793)
*Xu Yuan,Yi Wang,Zhuohang Jiang,Haohao Qu,Yujuan Ding,Shanru Lin,Guoliang Xing,Hongxia Yang,Jiannong Cao,Qing Li,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了智能眼镜从单纯的眼镜向“可穿戴人工智能平台”的转型，提出了AI智能眼镜的系统级定义，并从硬件基础、可穿戴智能、交互设计和应用场景四个维度进行系统梳理，最后指出了五大未来研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI技术的进步，智能眼镜正从第一人称捕捉和显示设备演变为受严格形态约束的可穿戴AI系统。目前缺乏一个统一的框架来组织这一新兴领域的技术、应用和挑战，因此需要系统性地界定和研究“AI智能眼镜”这一概念。

Method: 文章采用综述方法，围绕四个相互关联的维度对AI智能眼镜进行系统性研究：1）硬件基础（限制感知、计算和部署）；2）可穿戴智能（将第一人称信号转化为感知、情境和代理能力）；3）交互设计（用户如何请求、接收和调节协助）；4）应用场景（医疗、辅助、学习等如何重塑系统设计）。此外，识别了五个跨领域的研究挑战。

Result: 建立了以“可穿戴智能平台”为核心的统一框架，明确了AI智能眼镜作为系统集成概念的定义，涵盖了从底层硬件到上层应用的全链路设计要素，并归纳了特定领域需求对系统设计和评估的影响。

Conclusion: 该综述为AI智能眼镜这一新兴领域提供了统一的技术组织和挑战分析框架，强调了共设计（co-design）的重要性，并为未来的研究方向（如下一代硬件、可信智能、终身记忆等）指明了路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) are reshaping smart glasses from egocentric capture and display devices into platforms for wearable intelligence. Smart glasses increasingly serve as wearable AI systems that connect first-person observation with real-time assistance under strict form-factor constraints. We frame this transition through the lens of \emph{AI smart glasses} and define them as a system-level concept in which egocentric sensing, resource-aware computing, intelligent reasoning, multimodal interaction, and real-world application constraints are co-designed for personalized assistance in the physical world. To systematically study this perspective, we organize the survey around four connected dimensions. First, we examine the hardware foundation that bounds sensing, computation, feedback delivery, and sustained deployment. Second, we study wearable intelligence, where egocentric signals are transformed into perceptual, contextual, and agentic capabilities. Third, we discuss interaction design, through which users request, receive, correct, and regulate assistance during ongoing activity. Fourth, we analyze application scenarios across healthcare, accessibility, situated learning, daily life assistance, cultural tourism, and industrial support, showing how domain requirements reshape system design and evaluation. We further identify five cross-cutting research challenges for future AI smart glasses: next-generation hardware, trustworthy egocentric intelligence, lifelong personalized memory, proactive intelligence, and embodied foundation models. By centering smart glasses as wearable-intelligence platforms, this survey provides a unified framework for organizing technologies, applications, and open challenges in this emerging area.

</details>


### [33] [Absence is Presence: Understanding Visual Scene Negative Events Under Safety Cognitive Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19812)
*Zhiyun Jiang,Hanyong Wang,Binbin Liang,Yu Xie,Menglong Yang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRCD框架，通过反事实重建和对比解码解决视觉场景中的“负向描述”任务，即识别图像中本应存在但缺失的关键安全信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统场景理解仅关注图像中客观存在的肯定性信息，但在安全关键领域，识别“本应存在但实际缺失”的信息对于风险缓解至关重要。现有视觉语言模型因肯定性偏见、心理填充能力有限及表示偏差，难以有效推理此类否定性事件。

Method: 提出基于反事实重建和对比解码（CRCD）的负向描述框架：1) 将任务重构为反事实潜在变化描述以绕过肯定性偏见；2) 设计双分支反事实重建架构（模态补全分支恢复缺陷物体，功能关联分支推断完全缺失的安全物体）；3) 集成多条件表示学习机制，将通用特征投影到预定义的安全标准子空间以缓解表示偏差；4) 通过解码重建场景原型与原始输入之间的语义残差，激活解码器的否定逻辑。

Result: 实验验证了CRCD的有效性，为该开创性任务建立了高性能基线。

Conclusion: CRCD成功解决了将物理缺失转化为语义否定事件的挑战，提升了在安全约束下的视觉场景理解能力。

Abstract: Traditional scene understanding focuses on affirmative information objectively present in images. However, in safety-critical domains, comprehending key information that should exist but is actually absent is vital for risk mitigation. To bridge this gap, we focus on visual scene negative captioning with safety as the cognitive constraint. The core challenge is to convert physical absence into semantic negative events. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with this process because affirmation bias suppresses negative reasoning, while limited mental filling capability and representation bias further hinder the inference of absent information. To address these challenges, we propose a negative captioning framework based on counterfactual reconstruction and contrastive decoding (CRCD). Inspired by human cognition, CRCD reformulates the task as counterfactual latent change captioning to bypass affirmation bias. It contrasts a synthesized safe expectation with reality to identify semantic omissions. To address limited mental filling, we design a dual-branch counterfactual reconstruction architecture. The amodal completion branch restores defective objects, while the functional association branch infers completely absent safety objects. Concurrently, a multi-condition representation learning mechanism is integrated to mitigate representation bias by projecting universal features onto predefined safety criteria subspaces, thereby capturing information across more dimensions. By decoding feature-level semantic residuals between the reconstructed scene prototype and raw input, CRCD bounds the non-existence search space and activates the decoder's negative logic. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of CRCD, establishing a high-performance baseline for this pioneering task.

</details>


### [34] [SnapPhysics: A Physics-Aware Scene Graph from a Single View for Interactive Mixed Reality Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19815)
*Suji Kang,Seok-Young Kim,Young Bin Kim,Taewook Ha,Dieter Schmalstieg,Shohei Mori,Woontack Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SnapPhysics是一个无需训练的框架，通过单张图像重建3D物体并估计其物理属性（质量、摩擦、重心），结合3D重建与物理感知场景图，显著提升了混合现实中物理交互的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在混合现实(MR)中推断物理属性存在局限：基于视频动态分析计算成本高，而基于视觉语言模型(VLMs)的单图方法缺乏几何基础及物体间关系建模。

Method: 提出SnapPhysics框架，结合实例级3D重建与空间对齐，构建包含物体间关系和每物体度量几何结构的“物理感知场景图”，以此作为结构化上下文辅助VLM进行物理属性推理。

Result: 在3D-FRONT数据集上，场景级F-Score比最佳学习方法提升18.6%；在真实场景实验中，相比仅使用VLM的方法，平均绝对对数差误差(mALDE)降低高达20.5%，对数尺度相关性($r^2_{\mathrm{ls}}$)提升高达19.6%。

Conclusion: SnapPhysics实现了无需手动参数调优的物理交互式MR体验，有效解决了物理属性估计中的几何 grounding 和计算效率问题。

Abstract: We propose SnapPhysics, a training-free framework that reconstructs 3D objects and estimates their physical properties such as mass, friction, and center of gravity from a single image. For physically coherent interactions in mixed reality (MR), such properties are as important as geometry. Prior approaches infer them by analyzing object dynamics in video, which is computationally costly, or by querying vision-language models (VLMs) on single images, which lacks geometric grounding and inter-object relationships. We address these limitations by combining instance-level 3D reconstruction and spatial alignment with a physics-aware scene graph that encodes these relationships and per-object metric geometry as structured context for VLM-based property reasoning. Experiments on 3D-FRONT show that SnapPhysics improves scene-level F-Score by 18.6% over the best learning-based method, and on real captured scenes with ground-truth mass, it reduces the mean absolute log difference error (mALDE) by up to 20.5% and improves log-scale correlation ($r^2_{\mathrm{ls}}$) by up to 19.6% over VLM-only estimation. SnapPhysics enables physically interactive MR experiences without manual parameter tuning. Project page: https://snapphysics-ismar2026.github.io/.

</details>


### [35] [KoUniTalk: A Lightweight Articulation-Centered Korean-English 3D Talking Face Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19840)
*Hyunjung Chung,Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KoUniTalk，一个轻量级的韩英双语3D说话人脸基准，通过形变转移将不同拓扑的数据统一到共享网格，显著降低维度并促进跨语言评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
